*In school, back when I was younger, our teacher gave us each a flower seed.*
*But on our way back from school, as my younger twin sister was happily running around holding her seed, she fell down and lost her seed.*
*"Pain, pain, go away! Come again another day!" I chanted, trying to cast a magic spell on her scraped knee.*
*But between losing the flower seed in the fall and the pain of scraping her knee, my sister kept on crying.*
*Holding my crying sister's hand, I walked her back to our house, and I took her out back to the garden to plant our one remaining flower seed--the one I had been given.*
*After a few days, that one little seed had started growing into a pretty green plant with two budding leaves.*
Mom: "They look just like the two of you, Ichika, Futaba."
*Looking at the two leaves, thinking about what she meant, the two of us tilted our heads. Our mother laughed at us and said:*
Mom: "You two are twins, and that means that, just like these leaves, you two came from the same place."
Ichika: "What do you mean?"
Futaba: "I don't get it!"
Mom: "I suppose this is still a little difficult to understand, isn't it?"
*Tilting our heads even further, we began laughing along with our mother.*
Mom: "When the day comes that you two understand what I mean, make sure you remember this talk, okay?"
Mom: "Ichika is written as 'single fruit.' These two leaves, born from a 'single fruit,' are like the 'two leaves' in your name, Futaba."
*As she explained to us, Mom carefully wrote our names in the soil with the tip of her finger.*
Mom: "You two came to be from the same place. Just like this plant's single stem supporting two leaves, you should support each other and grow together."
Narrator: "Those were memories from long ago."
Narrator: "We repeated our mother's words countless times growing up."
Narrator: "Although our personalities were completely opposite, we were still identical twins."

*As Mom left me with those words, I sent her a sidelong glance and once again sprawled out on the bed.*
Futaba: "I know that."
*Mom had never said we weren't allowed to be close. I understood what she really wanted. But Ichika didn't seem to see things that way.*
*I'd snapped at Mom, but she wasn't the right person to talk to. Ichika was. Why was she avoiding me? Yelling at Mom wasn't going to get me an answer to that question.*
*Ever since Mom had told Ichika not to spoil me too much, Ichika had been looking a little sad. She'd been wearing an expression that told me she was wrestling with some difficult problem.*
*And then she'd started avoiding me. I didn't understand why...*
Futaba: "I really don't get what Ichika is thinking anymore..."
*When we were younger, I had always known.*
*The way Ichika preferred sitting in the house with a book over playing outside... Her shyness around people she didn't know...*
*I knew what I was good at and what I wasn't. I knew what she was good at and what she wasn't. In laughter and tears, we were always together. Always talking together. Because of that, I always knew.*
Futaba: "When it came to Ichika, I used to know everything..."
*But with the way things had become, Ichika wouldn't talk to me about anything.*
*I'd always intended to talk to Ichika about everything, but as we'd grown older, Ichika had closed off, stopped talking about herself.*
*Although her warmth and tenderness hadn't changed, I knew that a small gap between us had been forming for a while.*
*That gap made me sad. It made me want to be with Ichika more, to hold her more. But Ichika wouldn't say anything, so I really didn't know.*
*That sadness over our growing distance, and... one more thing that I had never talked to Ichika about mixed in my chest, leaving me feeling melancholy.*
*As I thought over it, a few tears slid down my cheek, and I wiped them away in a hurry with my pajama sleeve.*
Futaba: "Not yet. It's not like Ichika said she dislikes me or anything. I have to do something to catch her at school!"
Mom: "Futaba, you really will be late!"
Futaba: "Wha— Whoa, it's already 7:30?!"
*I shook off my worries and pulled myself together. While I did, Mom shouted at me again, a little angrily. I checked the time and confirmed I really was running that late, and I jumped out of bed.*
*As it turned out, by the time I'd changed and finished eating, it was already past 8 AM.*
Futaba: "I won't make it even if I run, so I might as well take it easy."
*Taking my time getting ready at home would have made Mom mad, so I'd pretended to rush out the door, but even if I'd kept that up I wouldn't have made it in time.*
*So I leisurely walked the usual road to school, alone.*
Futaba: "The autumn sun is kinda wimpy, isn't it? It's still warm, but the mornings are getting colder."
*I muttered to myself, and before I knew it, I recalled Ichika's warmth.*
*Actually, her hands were a little cool. In the summer, it always felt good when she would touch my cheek.*
*Whenever I told her that, she'd smile at me and say, "It's because you're usually much warmer than I am, just like a kid."*
*But if it got chilly out, she'd hold my hand, and we'd lean together and lock arms.*
*She'd smile gently and say, "You're so warm, Futaba."*
*Ichika's temperature was a bit lower than mine, but when we held hands or hugged each other close, our heat mixed together, and we would smile and laugh about how we both became warmer than we were on our own.*
*That's why walking alone right then felt... lonely.*
Futaba: "Even though we promised we'd always be together."
*Had Ichika forgotten that promise we'd made all those years ago?*
Futaba: "I could never forget."
*It had happened back when we were still very young. Mom had brought us to her friend's wedding.*
*Flowers and greenery surrounded a small church.*
*Mom's friend, the bride, wore a beautiful, white dress.*
*There was a girl, a little older than Ichika and me, grasping the hem of the bride's lovely dress, bawling.*
*She kept crying, "Big Sis, please don't go!"*
*I had learned that weddings were supposed to be happy, blessed occasions, so when I saw the girl crying, I tugged at my mother's skirt and asked her:*
Futaba: "Mom, getting married makes people happy, right?"
Mom: "That's right. Marriage means making a promise with the person you love to always stay together. I taught you that before, right?"
Futaba: "Yeah. I remembered that!"
Mom: "That's a good girl."
Futaba: "Ehehe! So then, why is that girl crying?"
Mom: "She's the bride's little sister, you see."
Futaba: "She's your friend's little sister?"
Mom: "That's right. And Futaba, what is Ichika?"
Futaba: "Ichika is my big sister!"
Mom: "Right! The bride is the crying girl's big sister, just like Ichika is your big sister."
Mom: "That big sister found a man she loves and has made a promise to be with him forever, which means she will have to leave her old home."
Futaba: "The girl's big sister is gonna go away?"
Mom: "Yes. She'll be leaving behind the house they grew up in and will move into a new house with her beloved. So that girl won't be able to see her big sis every day."
Futaba: "She won't? So then, if Ichika gets married, she'll go away too? I won't be able to see her?"
Mom: "That's right. When one of you falls in love with someone, that might happen."
Futaba: "I don't want that! I love Ichika, so I don't want her to disappear!"
Mom: "Oh?"
Futaba: "Yeah! Ichika, you love me too, right? You want to be with me forever, right?"
Ichika: "Yeah! I love you too, so I'll stay with you forever!"
Futaba: "Really?! Then, since we love each other, will you marry me and be with me forever, Ichika?"
Ichika: "Yeah, I promise!"
Futaba: "It's a promise!"
*The day we pinky swore, the day we made that promise, had been long ago. But I would always, always remember that promise.*
*Just a little while before, Mom's friend had come over to our house, bringing her own children. As they'd reminisced about that time, it had turned into a conversation about me and Ichika back then.*
Mom: "Watching you back then, your father said that even you two, who were so close, would at some point find the one you loved and go your separate ways, but..."
Mom: "...you two are still always together, even closer than you were back then."
*Mom's face was a mix of nostalgia and worry, but the two of us smiled, hearing about that day.*
Ichika: "That did happen, didn't it?"
Futaba: "I still love Ichika, and I still want to be with her forever."
*I had said that, beaming at Ichika, remembering, and she had given me a gentle smile in return.*
Ichika: "Yes, yes, I know. I love you too, Futaba."
Futaba: "Really?!"
Ichika: "Really. Besides, you wouldn't be able to do anything without me here, right?"
Futaba: "That's not true! I just don't do anything when you're watching!"
Ichika: "Hm? So if I went away, you could do everything perfectly fine on your own?"
Futaba: "You're not allowed to go away!"
Ichika: "Heheh. You're precious to me, Futaba, so don't worry, because I'm not going anywhere."
Futaba: "That's a promise, okay?!"
Ichika: "Yes, of course."
*I'd offered my pinky finger, just like I had done back at the wedding, and Ichika had giggled and twined her slender finger with mine.*
*Mom had just watched us, astonished, but... thinking back, that might have been what led to her confronting us the other day.*
Futaba: "Ichika didn't smile at me even once yesterday..."
*As the school gates came into view, I recalled how Ichika had acted the day before, and I felt stinging tears forming in the corners of my eyes.*
*When I'd cried the day before, it hadn't been Ichika who'd stroked my head gently...*
*...it had been Saki and Yuri.*
Saki: "I don't think Ichika has started to dislike you, Futaba."
Yuri: "Yeah, she probably just has some things she needs to think about. Give her some time."
*Saki had hugged me tight, while Yuri gently stroked my head.*
*Having the two girls I'd known since childhood hold me was pleasant and warm, but they were no replacement for Ichika.*
Futaba: "I wonder if she'll avoid me like yesterday. Will she run away from me?"
*As I murmured to myself, a tear fell down to the dry ground below.*
Futaba: "I can't help it. I love Ichika!"
*I loved her. I didn't know when that feeling had begun forming within me, but to me, Ichika had always been number one.*
*But my feelings were a little different from the usual love between twin sisters.*
*Mine was the kind of love found in the shoujo manga that Saki or the other members of the tennis club were always reading together, where the main character fell in love with a handsome young man.*
*Like the lovestruck girls in the cheesy romances Yuri told me about from time to time.*
Futaba: "I fell in love with Ichika."
*It was wrong. I knew that. Ichika and I were both girls, and twin sisters.*
*It was like those "boys love" manga the girls in my class made a fuss about.*
*Even a dummy like me knew that such a relationship wouldn't be accepted by society.*
Futaba: "I know that, but... I still love Ichika."
*I loved her. I wanted to kiss her, and hug her, and... to do even more things than that with her.*
*Even though I knew my wishes wouldn't ever be fulfilled, I couldn't just give up on Ichika!*
*Standing before the silent school gates, I took a deep breath.*
*The day before, Ichika wouldn't meet my eyes, even though I was crying.*
Futaba: "So should I really give up on her? Just throw away my romantic feelings?"
*I shook my head. Even if that feeling was wrong, I didn't want to imagine Ichika leaving me behind and spending her life with someone else.*
Futaba: "I love Ichika, even now. I love her more than anyone in the world."
*I raised my head and looked up at the school as I spoke.*
Futaba: "I won't let Ichika find anyone else to be with but me. There's no reason for us to avoid each other."
*I didn't think about stuff like the future, and I didn't really know what I'd have to do to achieve my goal. There were a lot of things I didn't know, but that didn't matter. I wasn't giving up on my love for Ichika!*
Futaba: "I'll be with Ichika forever, just like we promised. I won't ever give up."
*I walked down the hallway, quiet since classes were underway. I wanted to walk by Ichika's classroom, but my classroom was closer to the entrance than hers, so I gave up on that.*
Futaba: "Good morning."
Teacher: "Haahh... What are you doing, little Sakura? What time do you think it is right now? First period is almost over already, you realize."
Futaba: "E-Ehehe..."
Teacher: "You have some nerve, coming in late to your own homeroom teacher's class. Come to the staff room later."
Futaba: "Yes, sir."
*The teacher sighed at my grand entrance to class. You know, sure he also taught Ichika's class, but what was up with that nickname, "little Sakura?"*
*Watching the exchange between the teacher and me, and then me just standing there at the classroom door, everybody started giggling.*
Teacher: "Hurry up and take a seat."
*Urged by the teacher, I walked towards my seat, all the way at the back of the room.*
Classmate A: "Good morning, Futaba!"
Futaba: "Morning!"
Classmate B: "Did you sleep in?"
Futaba: "Kinda, yeah."
*I walked by my giggling peers, passing Saki's seat on the way to my own.*
Futaba: "Good morning, Saki!"
Saki: "Morning, Futaba... Are you okay?"
Futaba: "Yeah, I'm great!"
Teacher: "Hurry up and sit down, little Sakura!"
Futaba: "Yes, sir!"
*The teacher got mad again when I stopped to greet Saki, who was still looking a little worried, so I left it at:*
Futaba: "I'm just fine."
*Smiling again, I walked over and took my seat.*
*When second period came to an end and I got up from my seat to go to the staff room, Saki walked up to me.*
Saki: "Do you want me to come with you to the staff room?"
Futaba: "No, it's fine. It's finally break time, so you should take it easy. Besides, it's my fault for coming in late and all."
*I laughed, but Saki looked like she had reservations, her face still a little troubled.*
Saki: "Umm... Say... Futaba, are you really okay?"
Futaba: "Huh?"
Saki: "Umm, I just felt like, maybe... you know?"
Futaba: "Thanks, Saki, but don't worry, I'm fine!"
*I replied with a smile, but Saki still looked worried.*
Saki: "I see..."
*Looking like she still had something she wanted to say, Saki hesitated for a moment, and then she frowned, looking a little sad.*
*After Ichika had suddenly started avoiding me yesterday, I'd cried the whole time until I got home. Ichika wasn't there that day, either, and I'd had come to school alone.*
*I'd always been closest to Ichika, but Saki was my tennis partner, and we'd been friends since we were children.*
*I was sure Saki was worried about me. That was sweet, and I was grateful, but...*
*...I had to figure out how to handle my problems with Ichika on my own, somehow.*
*And those problems weren't something I could easily explain to Saki and Yuri, despite our closeness. I didn't think they'd get it...*
Futaba: "Like I told you, I'm fine! Well then, I'm going now!"
*I answered Saki's concerns with a smile and the most cheerful voice I could muster, then left the classroom.*
Teacher: "Honestly... two times makes a trend. This is the second time in a row you've come in late, you realize."
Futaba: "I'm sorry."
Teacher: "I don't expect you to be a straight-A student like your sister, but you at least need to get your act together."
Futaba: "Okaaay."
Teacher: "Could you at least try to take this seriously? Haahh... See if you can follow your sister's example a little more, and don't slack off."
*The teacher finished chastising me, and I left the staff room.*
*Thinking about it, people had always compared me and Ichika.*
*Ichika, the studious, unathletic one. Futaba, the spunky one who had trouble with school. We were twins, so we looked just the same, but our personalities were totally different.*
*We grew up always hearing Mom and everybody else telling us, "If you put those two halves together, you'll have one perfect person!"*
*And just like Mom had told us so long ago, we'd always worked to complement each other, to be each other's better half.*
*With Ichika not being naturally athletic, I had taught her how to swim, how to stretch properly, and stuff like that. In return, Ichika had helped me out tons of times with my studies.*
*Actually, at first they had told me that getting into our school would be impossible with my grades, but because we'd both wanted to go to the same school, we'd worked hard together.*
*The more I remembered, the more my desire to stay with Ichika forever grew.*
Futaba: "Yeah, from now on, I want us to be together forever."
*The truth was, I wanted to be even closer with her than we had been.*
*But I knew that suddenly making that change would be impossible. So for starters, I wanted to get us back to how things had been.*
Futaba: "Back to how we got along up until now."
*On my way returning to the classroom, I spotted Ichika walking next to Yuri.*
*When I saw them, my feet almost started moving on their own, propelling me in their direction.*
*But I somehow managed to hold myself back, and I stopped and thought for a moment.*
Futaba: "If I try to grab her like normal, she might just end up dodging me..."
*But when I heard myself say that, I shook my head back and forth, flustered.*
*I wasn't the smartest, so complicated things were tough for me, but even I could see that something was wrong with Ichika. So I thought, maybe if I just acted like always, Ichika would go back to her old self as well.*
*Fueled by that hope, I rushed towards Ichika to hug her like normal. However...*
*Ichika noticed me approaching and sidestepped.*
Futaba: "Ouchie..."
*Thinking I'd be able to latch on to Ichika like normal, I rushed at her at full speed, and when she avoided me I couldn't easily stop, so I went tumbling down the hallway.*
Yuri: "Futaba, you okay? Ooh, nice, you're wearing cute panties again today."
*Yuri helped me up while assessing my underwear.*
*Ichika looked at me for a second, but her face fell, and she went back to her classroom without saying anything.*
Futaba: "If I try to grab her like normal, she might just end up dodging me."
*I stealthily moved around behind Ichika, stepping delicately to avoid making any sound. If I just ran up behind her like usual, she'd hear my footsteps.*
*But if I snuck up on her and hugged her tightly, I figured I could get her.*
*Everyone stared as I crept up on Ichika. Just before I reached her, Yuri turned to look at me.*
*Silently giving thanks to Yuri for pretending not to notice, I was just about to hug Ichika from behind, when...*
Ichika: "Oh, someone dropped something."
Futaba: "Whoa!"
*Just when I was within reach, Ichika suddenly squatted down to pick up something, resulting in me flailing around and falling down when I tried to grab on to her.*
*Yuri, who had been watching the whole thing, looked at me and mouthed, "Tough luck," as Ichika stood back up and looked at me somewhat sadly.*
Ichika: "Futaba, do you need something?"
Futaba: "Umm... No, nothing in particular."
Ichika: "I see. The bell's about to ring, so you should go back to your classroom as well."
*I answered with a nod, and Ichika stepped into her classroom. That hadn't been her normal, kind voice. Her tone was distant, detached.*
*Hearing her talk like that to me almost pushed me to tears, but if I cried then and there, Ichika wouldn't think much of it at all, and that thought scared me.*
Yuri: "Futaba..."
Futaba: "...I'm not... crying."
Yuri: "I see. That's a good girl, Futaba. There, there."
Futaba: "Yeah."
*As Yuri smiled gently and patted my head, it once again felt like my tears would start flowing any second, but Yuri took out her handkerchief and touched it to my eyes, which helped me just barely endure.*
Yuri: "Futaba, you were late again this morning, right? If you're late to class another time today, you might get in trouble."
Futaba: "Yeah..."
Yuri: "Let's talk with Saki again at lunch. Now, how about heading back to your classroom?"
Futaba: "Okay."
*I nodded my thanks to Yuri for her kindness and went back to my own classroom.*
*But during the following periods, the way Ichika had spoken to me wouldn't leave my mind.*
*Thanks to that, my pop quiz went by in a blur...*
Saki: "Futaba, aren't you done clearing away your stuff yet? It's already lunchtime."
Futaba: "Oh, right!"
Saki: "It's fine, you don't have to rush."
*As I sat there spacing out, thinking about Ichika, lunchtime soon rolled around. After Saki snapped me out of my daze, I quickly got my things cleaned up.*
*The two of us brought our lunch to Ichika's classroom, but as I expected, only Yuri was there.*
Futaba: "Yuri, Ichika's missing again today?"
Yuri: "Yup. She just hurried away, claiming she had some student council work to take care of again today."
Futaba: "You don't believe her?"
Yuri: "She's never had to do that stuff during lunchtime before, right? Plus, she took her lunch with her when she left."
Futaba: "I wonder if she's eating lunch by herself somewhere..."
Yuri: "If she's eating lunch outside the classroom, there aren't a lot of places she could be."
Saki: "The courtyard, and the cafeteria. Aside from that, I suppose there are also the roof and the terrace."
*If those were the only places she could be, then I had a good chance of finding her. Yeah, it didn't even matter how many people there were.*
Futaba: "No doubt about it, I can find her."
Saki: "Hm? Did you say something, Futaba?"
Futaba: "Nothing."
Yuri: "Hey, how long are you two gonna stand there? Come sit down."
Saki: "Right."
*Urged by Yuri, Saki sat down.*
Saki: "Futaba, what's wrong? Aren't you going to sit?"
Futaba: "I really want to eat with Ichika, so I'm going to go look for her."
Yuri: "I see... Well, lunch always tastes better when all four of us are eating together, so I approve."
Saki: "Me too, of course. Oh, but are you going to be okay on your own? Do you want the two of us to come with you?"
Futaba: "Thanks, you two, but I'll be fine. I have to hurry and look for Ichika, so you guys can go ahead and start without us."
Yuri: "Try not to run in the halls, Futaba. I'm sure Ichika would just get mad at you again if she saw that."
Futaba: "Yeah, got it, Yuri."
Saki: "Even if you can't find Ichika, you need to eat properly, so don't take too long. Also, don't be in such a rush that you fall, alright?"
Futaba: "Okay! Thanks, Saki. I'm heading out!"
*After talking with the two of them, I left the classroom.*
*Lunchtime. Since everyone is eating, the usual crowds swarming the hallway have died down a bit, and there aren't very many people around.*
*Yuri had told me not to, but I couldn't hold back and ended up running down the hallway. On the way, a teacher spotted me.*
Teacher: "You again, little Sakura? I've told you not to run in the hallways like that!"
Futaba: "Sorry, Teach, but I'm in a hurry! Later!"
Teacher: "H-Hey!"
*Evading my upset teacher, I went to check the spots Yuri and Saki had suggested.*
*Finally, I came to a stop in front of the student council room.*
Futaba: "Is it locked?"
*I pushed my head up against the door, checking whether it was open.*
*In the courtyard, everyone had been sprawled out happily eating. I'd never eaten in the cafeteria, but the menu had been filled with affordable and tasty-sounding dishes. The rooftop had been covered in greenery, like a garden.*
*The terrace had looked like an elegant place to have lunch. And although you couldn't eat there, I had even looked in the library, where Ichika often went.*
*I'd checked all of those places, but I hadn't found Ichika.*
Futaba: "Ichika, why can't I find you?"
*My loneliness growing, tears forming in my eyes, I pressed my cheek against the door in front of me, as if doing so might somehow hold back the tears.*
*A breeze blew past, pleasant on my body, which was flushed from running.*
*The student council room was a little ways from most of the classrooms, so people usually didn't go that way unless they had some business there. As a result, not a lot of people were around at that time of day.*
*I was a little grateful for that—I didn't want anyone to see me feeling so down.*
*Yuri and Saki still hadn't eaten their lunches and had been waiting for me in the classroom.*
Yuri: "I told you not to run, but you ran anyways, didn't you? Your hair's a mess. Come here, I'll fix it for you."
Saki: "You couldn't find Ichika, huh?"
Futaba: "No..."
Saki: "It's okay! If we just wait this out, I'm sure we'll all be eating together again soon. Right, Yuri?"
Yuri: "Yup, yup! Ichika's been acting a little strange lately. I'll help look into it too, and then we'll have Futaba right as rain in no time. And, there, nice and tidy again."
Saki: "Shall we eat?"
*Yuri finished fixing my hair and patted my head once, and Saki laughed and set her lunch on the table.*
Yuri: "Alright, Futaba! Since you worked hard, I'm going to give you this specially-made omelet as a reward! It's so good, I bet your jaw will drop!"
Futaba: "It's that good?!"
Saki: "Hahah. Yuri, I think you're over-selling them, but it's true, your omelets are delicious. They're sweet and fluffy."
Yuri: "Alright, Futaba. Say, 'Aaaah.'"
Futaba: "Aaaah."
*Just like Ichika always did, Yuri fed me a piece of her omelet.*
*It was sweet and fluffy, but...*
Saki: "Was Yuri's cooking so good it made you cry?"
*Saki smiled as she spoke, and she wiped away my tears with her handkerchief.*
*The omelet's sweet taste and Yuri and Saki's kindness mingled in my heart, and I couldn't stop the tears.*
*Even so, I laughed.*
Futaba: "Ehehe. Yeah, it was so good it made me cry."
Yuri: "Wow, I really am a great chef."
Saki: "Yuri, don't get carried away."
Yuri: "But it's true."
Saki: "I'm the one who taught you how to make that, remember?"
Yuri: "Ugh... Saki, you promised you wouldn't tell anybody."
*Watching them go on like that made me laugh.*
*I sniffled, and the sweet omelet blended with the taste of my salty tears.*
*Yeah, what good would it do, with the way she was avoiding me?*
*I could tell from the way she was acting during recess before, but...*
Futaba: "She doesn't have to be this blunt about avoiding me..."
Saki: "I agree..."
Yuri: "Honestly, it seems like Ichika is really serious about this."
*They both replied as I mumbled to myself.*
*I brought out my lunchbox and opened it. Inside I found both carrots and peppers, which I didn't like, and I started prodding them with my chopsticks.*
*Ichika wasn't there to tell me, "You have to be strong and eat them all," like usual.*
*Looking at the empty seat next to me made me feel like crying. I shoved all the carrots and peppers into my mouth at once and started chewing.*
*The bitter peppers and the sweet carrots mixed. But even though I did good and ate them all, Ichika wasn't there to pat my head.*
*When my tears were about to overflow, Saki started gently stroking my hair.*
Saki: "Even though Ichika isn't here, you ate them all. What a good girl."
Futaba: "!"
*The shape and feel of the hand on my head was different from Ichika's, but as Saki reassured me and patted me, the warm, gentle feeling broke my remaining resistance, and the tears started flowing.*
*Once they started, it wasn't easy stopping them again. Every time Saki patted my head and consoled me, more tears flowed down my cheeks and landed in my lunchbox.*
*Yuri brought out her handkerchief and smiled.*
Yuri: "Futaba, your food's gonna get salty."
Futaba: "Yeah."
Yuri: "Are you lonely because Ichika isn't here?"
Futaba: "Yeah..."
Yuri: "Saki and I feel lonely too when the two of you aren't together."
Saki: "Yeah, we really do."
Yuri: "Futaba, do you think Ichika doesn't like you anymore?"
Futaba: "No, I don't. Ichika is just acting weird right now."
Yuri: "Then, do you know why she's avoiding you?"
Futaba: "I don't know for sure, but I have a general idea."
Yuri: "Then it'll be fine. As long as you hang in there, I'm sure Ichika will give in soon enough."
Futaba: "She will?"
Saki: "Yes. I can tell that she's been having a hard time of things too, ever since this all started yesterday."
Futaba: "She has?"
Yuri: "Yes. Saki and I were talking about how she's been a little off for the last couple days. Even we can tell, and we aren't as close to her as you are, so it's definitely true."
Futaba: "I see..."
Saki: "Right. So, come on."
*Saki leaned over to hug me tightly, and Yuri reached across the table to pat me.*
Yuri: "You can just go right back to your usual, lively self, okay?"
Saki: "But if you feel like crying, tell me, okay? I'll lend you my chest to cry in whenever you need."
*Saki pulled me even closer when she said that, and my chest tightened, making me cry even more.*
*But then...*
Yuri: "What?! Saki's boobs are mine! I don't care if Futaba's crying, I can't let her have them!"
Saki: "Y-Yuri! What are you saying?!"
Yuri: "Huh? They're not mine?"
Saki: "I have no idea what you're talking about!"
*As their banter wrapped around me, before I knew it, my tears had stopped.*
*Afternoon classes had ended. I had cleaning duty, so while taking out the trash I stopped by Ichika's classroom, but she wasn't there. On my way back, I ran into Ichika on her way to the student council room.*
Futaba: "Ichika, do you have student council work to do?"
Ichika: "I do... See you later."
*We only exchanged a few words, but she still had a bitter look on her face, and she wouldn't make eye contact.*
*But as I watched her walk away, I realized something.*
Futaba: "Ichika isn't avoiding me. She's running away."
*As she'd turned away, our eyes had met for a second.*
*Her expression was bitter, but looking in her eyes, I could tell that, more than anything, she was sad.*
*I'd been with her forever, and I'd wanted to be with her forever since we were kids.*
*So I could tell, just from that fleeting eye contact, that she was sad. That helped me to better understand what was going on.*
Futaba: "Ichika really does want us to be close."

Futaba: "Are you really okay with that, Ichika?"
Ichika: "The feelings we have for each other now are just the result of being sisters and spending our whole lives together. We simply mistook that fondness for being in love."
Futaba: "Wai—"
*Ichika left the room right as I started talking again. The door shut in front of my eyes, and a few tears ran down my cheeks.*
*I slowly got ready and headed downstairs, feeling down the entire time. I walked into the living room and realized Ichika had already left for school—just like she'd said she would.*
*We happened to run into each other in the halls, but Ichika quickly turned away.*
*At lunch, Ichika wasn't in her classroom when I headed over, and after school, she'd already left by the time my club practice finished.*
Futaba: "She even ate dinner without me..."
*When I'd come home, Ichika had already eaten dinner, finished bathing, and shut herself in her room. I'd tried knocking on her door, but she hadn't answered.*
*Faced with that silence, I couldn't work up the courage to open her door.*
*Would she be gone again the next morning? Would she hide from me again the next night?*
*If things continued like this, I wouldn't see Ichika at all, even though we lived in the same house.*
*I wouldn't be able to say "good morning" to her when we first woke, or even talk to her at all... My heart broke to think of it.*
Futaba: "Even though she said she loves me..."
*The day before, Ichika had told me that we weren't actually in love, that we were just misunderstanding our feelings because we had always been together. That wasn't true at all!*
Futaba: "After all, if that were true, it wouldn't hurt this much."
*We wanted to be together, but somehow, we had ended up drifting further apart. I wasn't going to give up!*
Futaba: "I know it's wrong, but she told me that she loves me."
*We'd even had sex, if only once. Ichika wasn't putting up a front. When she'd said she loved me, she was telling me how she truly felt. I was sure of it!*
*Ever since last night, I just couldn't figure out what she was thinking.*
*Ichika was much smarter than me, so I didn't think I'd be able to understand the complicated things going through her head even if she explained them to me.*
*But because of that uncertainty, I couldn't give up yet!*
Futaba: "For starters, I have to convince her this wasn't just a misunderstanding! Hwaaah..."
*I'd never spent so long thinking hard about something, and I let out a huge yawn. I checked the time. Already past midnight.*
Futaba: "Even though I've been thinking so long, I still don't know what to do. Maybe I'll just call it a night."
*Just thinking wasn't getting anywhere, so I decided to start watching Ichika closely and see if I learned anything. That decided, I drifted off to sleep.*
Futaba: "Good night, Ichika."
*Before I fell asleep, I whispered those words.*
*A few days later. I had become Ichika's stalker.*
*Well, "stalker" wasn't quite right. I mean, I already went looking for Ichika whenever she wasn't near, so it wasn't like I was doing anything particularly different.*
Futaba: "This isn't any different from what I did when we were kids! Probably..."
Yuri: "So you've been sneaking around and spying on people ever since childhood, Futaba?"
*Stiffening in surprise, I turned around to find Saki and Yuri.*
Futaba: "Huh?! I was sneaking?"
Yuri: "Try taking a look at yourself in a mirror. You're tiptoeing around and hiding behind stuff, and you jumped a foot in the air when I talked to you."
Saki: "Futaba, were you really sneaking?"
Futaba: "W-Well... I mean... She almost caught me, so..."
Yuri: "You mean Ichika?"
Futaba: "Yeah."
Saki: "And would it be bad if she caught you?"
Futaba: "Hmm... I dunno. She's trying really hard to avoid me right now."
*I gave a sad little laugh, and Yuri for some reason started smacking my head.*
Yuri: "Yeah, that's a good girl. You're a brave girl, Futaba, so you deserve a good headpat."
Futaba: "You aren't patting me, you're hitting me!"
Yuri: "You're gonna end up even shorter!"
Futaba: "You meanie!"
Yuri: "It's okay, girls are cuter when they're smaller, you know."
Futaba: "...Well, alright then."
Saki: "Yuri, stop joking around, and Futaba, don't just go along with her."
*Saki scolded us, and I giggled. I loved Yuri and Saki—no matter what was going on, they always made me laugh.*
*Even in my grave situation, with Ichika avoiding me, the two of them could get me back to my usual, upbeat self in no time.*
Yuri: "Seems like Little Miss Ichika has started avoiding you even more."
Saki: "Did something happen last weekend?"
Futaba: "Yeah..."
*Even if they were my best friends, I couldn't really tell them that Ichika and I slept together that weekend.*
*Even though I didn't go into details, the two of them looked at me with concern, and I had to fight to hold back the tears that started welling up.*
Saki: "Futaba, did you try out our secret plan?"
Futaba: "Yeah."
*The secret plan was something Yuri and Saki thought of for me and Ichika to make up.*
*I'd been feeling down because of Ichika avoiding me, so they'd come up with something together.*
*Ichika really did love me. She was just putting distance between us because she felt like she had to. Therefore, I should tell her exactly how I felt!*
*So I did just like they said to: I laid my feelings bare before Ichika. I showed her all of me.*
*But I couldn't exactly tell them everything about how it went.*
Yuri: "Hmm... So it didn't work, huh? Ichika really does love you, so I thought you'd be able to win her over if you used the right approach."
Futaba: "'Win her over,' seriously?"
*I laughed it off, but in my mind, I agreed. After all, Ichika really had told me that she loved me.*
Saki: "Ichika can be severe and stubborn at times, can't she?"
Yuri: "Stiff as steel."
Futaba: "Yeah."
*We all nodded and laughed.*
*Ichika really was stubborn, and she had been ever since we were little.*
Yuri: "But you know, when she saw how sad you were because of her avoiding you, she'd always get worried."
Futaba: "Really?"
Yuri: "Yep. She's asked me about you several times already."
*I didn't know that. So Ichika did feel bad about it!*
Saki: "Ichika is really diligent. She's probably just doing what your mother told her to because she thinks she has to."
Futaba: "Yeah."
Saki: "I think you just need to hang on for a bit longer."
*Saki gently stroked my hair, but as she did, I couldn't help but think, "This is totally different from when Ichika does it."*
*That's how much I looooved Ichika. Just thinking about her made my chest tighten up enough to hurt a little.*
*But I couldn't let things keep going like that. How could I make Ichika notice me? I had to figure something out...*
*I spent a week stalking Ichika.*
*During breaks, I would roam the building searching for her. When her class had P.E., I would just stare at her out the window.*
*During that time, I noticed one thing.*
Futaba: "Our eyes keep meeting. That's not just my imagination, right?"
*I was in class, but I was completely ignoring it. Instead I was whispering to myself as I watched the sports grounds, where Ichika was exercising.*
*We went to the same school, and our classrooms were on the same floor. It would have been impossible not to run into each other.*
*I spent time hanging around in the hallways, looking for her, and sometimes I'd run into her.*
*And I was absolutely certain. Every time she saw me, our eyes would meet.*
*It wasn't like I started shouting her name whenever I ran into her. I just looked. But even so...*
Futaba: "Oh."
*I'd been watching Ichika the whole time as she ran around on the sports grounds.*
Futaba: "She just looked over here, didn't she?"
*Even in situations like that, where I was watching her from afar, our eyes had met a number of times.*
*Ichika knew very well which classroom I'd be in and that I wasn't going anywhere.*
*Ignoring the class, which I really should have been paying attention to, I kept my eyes firmly on Ichika.*
*Even though she knew I was going to be in that same seat in that same class for the rest of the period, she kept glancing my way, over and over, though whenever our eyes met, she'd start to look awkward.*
Futaba: "Maybe... Maybe this is a sign of Ichika worrying about me, like Yuri mentioned earlier."
*If Ichika really was worried about my being down from her ignoring me, then...*
Futaba: "I wonder if I could find a way to make Ichika pay more attention to me."
*I muttered to myself as I thought about it, still ignoring the lecture. I wasn't smart like Ichika. I was just an idiot with rather average grades...*
*...but even so, I had to think of something, if I wanted to spend the rest of my life with my beloved Ichika.*
*Think, think, think with all my might! And suddenly, I thought of something I could actually pull off.*
Futaba: "Oh yeah! All I have to do is make Ichika worry about me even more."
*I clapped my hands together, pleased. If Ichika was already worried about me, I just had to push her a bit further.*
*Yuri had said that Ichika was worried at seeing me looking sad all the time.*
*Okay, so how should I make myself look even more glum?*
Futaba: "I'm finished."
Mom: "But Futaba, there's still a lot left on your plate."
Futaba: "Huh? O-Oh, yeah..."
*When I set down my chopsticks, Mom spoke up, sounding worried. I was so surprised that I responded a little strangely, but hopefully that just made me seem out of it.*
Mom: "Are you feeling alright? You've been doing this for a few days now."
Futaba: "I'm just feeling a little under the weather."
*Picking up my plate, I left my seat and headed for the kitchen.*
*On my way there, I snuck a glance at Ichika, who was still in the middle of eating.*
*She didn't say anything, but just like I'd hoped, she was watching me with concern.*
Mom: "Can we do anything for you? Maybe some medicine for your stomach?"
*I shook my head violently, remembering the stinky stomach medicine. I mean, I wasn't really sick, so I didn't actually need any medicine.*
Futaba: "I-I'm fine. Well, since I'm a little out of it, I'm going to go to bed."
*It would've been a disaster if they'd figured out I wasn't actually sick, so I needed to get out of there, quick.*
Mom: "If you're still feeling sick later, I'm going to take you to the hospital, alright?"
Futaba: "Nah, it's really not that bad."
*I hurried out of the living room, trying to get away from Mom's concern.*
Futaba: "Whew... That was close."
*I returned to my room, sighing in relief once the door closed behind me.*
Futaba: "Ichika was watching me today too!"
*I smiled a little to myself as I sat down on the bed. The sadder I acted, the more Ichika would worry about me. The more she worried, the closer she'd watch me.*
*When I thought along those lines, I'd quickly figured out a plan:*
*Don't finish my meals.*
Futaba: "I pretty much always eat every scrap on my plate, even when I'm feeling sick."
*Normally, no matter how bad I'd been feeling, I'd always eaten all my food. It didn't matter what else was going on—dinner was delicious! Oh... Except when it was something I didn't like.*
*Even that one time when I'd been bedridden with a cold, Ichika had said:*
Ichika: "Futaba, you've still got a healthy appetite even when you're running a fever, huh?"
*She'd been amazed, but it didn't matter how sick I was, I never missed a meal!*
Futaba: "I never leave behind food. Ichika knows that."
*I grinned and rolled over on my bed, but then my tummy growled loudly at me. It hurt a bit...*
Futaba: "I'm so hungry... but this is for the best!"
*I rubbed my tummy, which felt completely flat. Ever since I'd started not finishing my meals, Ichika had started eating dinner with me.*
*In the morning we still ate separately, since by the time I was awake Ichika was already gone, but in the evening we ate together.*
*We didn't talk to each other like we had in the past, but when Ichika had noticed that I barely ate any food and ate really slowly as if I was totally out of it, she'd looked worried.*
*I was betting that Mom had said something to Ichika about my sudden lack of appetite.*
*Plus, I was even leaving food uneaten at lunch, so Saki and Yuri might have said something to Ichika too.*
*I didn't know what they were telling her, but Ichika was clearly looking more and more worried by the day whenever she glanced my way.*
Futaba: "Walking around with an empty stomach all day is kinda uncomfortable, but I've got to stay strong!"
*I stroked my noisy stomach one more time, trying to stop it from rumbling, and closed my eyes.*
*I was working hard on a lot of fronts, trying to make Ichika worry about me. Not only was I eating less, I was even avoiding the sweets I enjoyed so much.*
Futaba: "I've got to hang in there... If I can stay strong a little bit longer, she'll give in and start paying more attention to me."
*Chanting that incantation to myself every night, I pushed myself hard day by day, trying to make Ichika worry about me, and sure enough, my efforts paid off and Ichika looked my way more and more.*
*When I pretended to be sick, Ichika would start worrying about me, watching me like a hawk. That made me incredibly happy...*
*...but I was starting to feel pretty exhausted.*
*That day, I'd been feeling out of it ever since I'd woken up.*
*Probably because I didn't sleep well at night, spending every night hungry, my stomach sometimes felt like it was curling in on itself, and it hurt a lot. I didn't really know why.*
Saki: "Futaba, are you okay?"
Futaba: "Wha?"
*It was lunchtime and I was sitting at my seat, dozing. Saki walked up and crouched down, looking at my face.*
Saki: "You look terrible. Did you not eat lunch today either?"
Futaba: "No... But I'm fine."
Saki: "You don't look fine to me! Come on, here's some candy. Say, 'Aaah.'"
Futaba: "I don't want it."
*The smell of the candy made my tummy growl, but... I couldn't take it. If I ate it, I was sure I wouldn't be able to control myself later on.*
*I'd been working so hard. I couldn't give in. I shook my head at Saki, refusing.*
Saki: "Futaba..."
*Saki watched me sadly.*
Futaba: "Sorry... I really don't want it."
*That was a lie, of course. I wanted it so bad.*
*But what I wanted even more was for Ichika to pay attention to me. I had to stay strong.*
Yuri: "Wait, seriously? Futaba's so sick she can't even eat candy?"
Saki: "Oh, Yuri."
*From the corner of my eye, I noticed two people walking up next to Saki. I looked up to see Yuri, and... Ichika.*
Yuri: "Hey, Futaba. Ichika told me you haven't been eating dinner or even any sweets lately. She said she was worried and wanted to come check on you."
*Yuri's voice came to me faintly, as if from a great distance. Ichika was... worried?*
Yuri: "Saki and I'd already seen you weren't eating lunch here at school. What's wrong, Futaba?"
Futaba: "Nothing's... wrong."
Saki: "Something is definitely wrong! You've been looking kind of thin recently, and at club practice, I'm afraid you're going to collapse."
Futaba: "Like I said, I'm fine..."
*I answered their worries with a smile. But then...*
Ichika: "...Futaba, please, won't you tell us what's wrong? Does it hurt somewhere?"
Futaba: "...!"
*Ichika was looking at me, not turning away. Ichika, who hadn't spoken to me in weeks except for the few times when she'd had to.*
*She looked worried. Really, really worried. And a little sad.*
Futaba: "Why...?"
Ichika: "Futaba?"
Futaba: "Ichika... why... are... you... so...?"
Ichika: "Futaba?!"
*Ichika's warm voice came to me, filled with worry, and the world started spinning.*
*Pain shot through my back and I looked up at Ichika. There were tears in her eyes.*
*The world around me went dark.*
*The first thing I heard was the ticking of a clock. I opened my eyes to a white ceiling. Sunlight peeked into the room through white curtains.*
*And then:*
Ichika: "...Are you awake?"
*I turned to face that voice full of concern, and sure enough, there was Ichika, looking worried.*
Futaba: "Ichika..."
Ichika: "Do you feel alright? You passed out in the classroom."
Futaba: "I... passed out?"
Ichika: "Yes."
*Ichika explained it all so simply that, even in my daze, I could understand.*
*Ichika looked at me with concern and a touch of sadness.*
*She was sitting a little ways away from the bed, looking right at me.*
Ichika: "Futaba, you haven't been eating all your dinner lately, right?"
*Ichika spoke slowly, like she'd thought hard about exactly what to say. Back when we'd simply spent all our time together happily, Ichika had never talked that way.*
*What's more, usually when I got sick she would instantly rush to sit beside my bed, clasping my hand.*
Ichika: "Futaba, are you alright? Does it hurt anywhere?"
*And all the while, she would gently stroke my cheek. But this time...*
Ichika: "I was so surprised when you fainted. Do you really feel that bad?"
Futaba: "Not... at... all."
Ichika: "Do you mean it? Futaba, I know something's wrong."
Futaba: "I really... feel fine."
*I could tell that she was worried, and hearing her tender voice again was wonderful, but even so, the way she was acting felt so distant.*
*Ichika had sat far enough away that, even if I'd reached out to her, I couldn't touch her. Ichika had no intention of coming into contact with me.*
Ichika: "Futaba, you haven't been eating properly for over a week now. I know that's not normal for you."
Futaba: "I guess you're right..."
*Her worry for me was obvious, but her voice still sounded somehow detached.*
*I just knew she was concerned, but she was still holding herself so far away from me.*
*Like she was afraid of getting hurt if she got close. Like the two of us being close was a bad thing.*
Ichika: "If you're that unwell, we should go to the hospital."
Futaba: "No, I'm fine."
Ichika: "But at this rate, you might faint again."
Futaba: "I told you, I'm fine!"
Ichika: "!"
*She was listening attentively, putting on a show of worrying, and yet she wouldn't meet my eyes. I found myself shouting at her in irritation.*
Futaba: "Whatever. It's not like you even care about me, right?"
*I just didn't understand. How could she act like that?*
*I was in love with Ichika. I wanted to be with her forever.*
*Ichika had even told me that she was in love with me, but then she'd gone right back to avoiding me.*
*She'd started worrying about me again, but she was still keeping her distance, and she wouldn't look me in the eyes... She wouldn't let herself be close to me...*
*With her acting like that, there was no way I could figure out what she actually thought about me, which drove me crazy. What was I supposed to do?!*
Ichika: "Of course I care! We're sisters, and we go to the same school."
Futaba: "You've been avoiding me this whole time! So go on, go home without me like you always do!"
Ichika: "That has nothing to do with this. Besides, there's no way I'm going to leave you here like this."
Futaba: "I don't care! I can get home just fine on my own! I don't need your help!"
Ichika: "I can't just leave my sister at school after she fainted. Mom told me to walk home with you."
Futaba: "Mom again? You don't care about me unless she tells you to, huh?"
Ichika: "What are you saying?"
*It was unlike any fight we'd had before.*
*After all, Ichika was always nice to me. Because she was so sweet, she'd never gotten mad at me.*
*But Ichika had started looking angry, growing irritated.*
*I understood... I understood, of course.*
*We were twins after all.*
*We'd been together our whole lives.*
*Of course Ichika was getting irritated, just like I was.*
*But I had no idea what I was supposed to do about it. What was I supposed to do...?*
Futaba: "You just don't care about me anymore, do you?!"
Ichika: "What are you—"
Futaba: "Am I wrong?! If I oversleep and come in late, if I forget to do my homework... doesn't matter what's happening to me, you won't care at all, right?!"
Ichika: "That's completely unrela—"
Futaba: "You don't even care that I'm sick!"
Ichika: "..."
Futaba: "Even though I'm so ill I passed out... you won't hold my hand..."
Ichika: "I..."
Futaba: "You may act like you're worried, but you won't do a single kind thing for me!"
Ichika: "That's neither here nor there. Right now we need to figure out how to help you get over whatever it is that's making you feel so sick that you can't eat."
Futaba: "Stop pretending like you care! It doesn't matter to you whether I eat or not!"
Ichika: "How can you say that?"
Futaba: "I can say it because it's true! You avoid me, you won't come near me, you don't pay attention to me... You and I are practically strangers now!"
Ichika: "It's not like I enjoy acting like that!"
Futaba: "Liar!"
Ichika: "I'm not lying! I'm doing it all for you, so that you can learn to be properly independent!"
Futaba: "..."
Ichika: "I'm doing this because you're important to me, because I love you!"
*I'd never heard Ichika yell at me like that, and I froze up.*
*I didn't know she felt that way... But, if that was how she felt, then...*
*If she could look at me with tears in her eyes and say that she loved me, then why...?*
Futaba: "If you really think that, then why don't you want to be with me?"
*The words I'd longed to say; the words I'd longed to hear.*
*I loved Ichika, and she loved me.*
*Even so, she'd said being together was wrong.*
*Even though we loved each other.*
*Even though we were important to each other.*
*Why?*
*It felt like ripping my heart out, uttering those words that had been burning within me for so long. Tears streamed down my face.*
Futaba: "We love each other... so why... can't we... be together?"
Ichika: "Futaba..."
*Ichika didn't know how to handle my crying.*
Futaba: "This whole time, I've been watching you... just hoping that... maybe you would look at me by accident..."
Ichika: "I truly love you, Futaba, so I can't help but be worried about you."
Futaba: "So, I figured maybe if you thought I was sick... then you'd be there for me, like you used to."
Ichika: "..."
Ichika: "...Is that the reason you haven't been eating lately?"
Futaba: "Yeah."
*I nodded.*
Ichika: "You stopped eating just to get my attention?"
Futaba: "Yes, because I like you... I love you! I only wanted you to pay attention to me again!"
Ichika: "Futaba..."
Futaba: "I want to... be with you... I want to... always, always be with you... Waaaaaaaaaaah!"
*I just wanted her to understand that. I just wanted her to know how much I loved her.*
*And I wanted her to know how much I wanted to be with her.*
*I wanted to be together with Ichika... I wanted to always, always be together with her.*
*It hurt... My tears wouldn't stop... The infirmary bed was already damp with tears, but I kept crying.*
*Ichika scooted towards me, closer and closer. She let out a small sigh and said:*
Ichika: "You are hopeless."
*Murmuring that, Ichika enfolded me in a warm hug.*
Ichika: "Dummy. There's no way I'd leave you alone."
Futaba: "But..."
Ichika: "Do you remember what they used to say about our names?"
*Ichika pulled away from me a bit and wiped my tears with her handkerchief.*
Futaba: "Huh?"
Ichika: "Our names. Ichika means 'one fruit,' and from a single fruit seed, a plant with 'two leaves' sprouted: Futaba."
Futaba: "Oh..."
Ichika: "You remember Mom telling us that a long time ago, right? We're twins, so the meanings of our names are connected."
Futaba: "Yeah."
*It was something our mother had repeated over and over since we'd been little.*
Ichika: "She used to say, 'You're twins: you came into the world together, and you'll always be connected to each other.'"
Futaba: "And then she'd always say, 'Make up for what the other lacks, help one another, and grow together.'"
Ichika: "That's right. Futaba, you were born alongside me. You are my other half. Leaving you behind would mean leaving behind a part of myself."
Futaba: "Yeah."
Ichika: "I was so stupid, avoiding my precious other half. That's no way to grow up. I felt lost and hurt, but worst of all, I made my beloved Futaba sad."
Futaba: "Ichika..."
Ichika: "The two of us just can't take being apart."
Futaba: "Yeah!"
*Ichika smiled gently and patted my head, like she'd used to. It made me so happy I couldn't help but cry a little.*
*But that was fine, because my precious Ichika was there with a smile to wipe away my tears.*
Ichika: "I'm really sorry, Futaba, for making you so sad."
*Ichika wiped away my tears with her handkerchief, and she gently kissed the corners of my eyes, hot from crying.*
*After that, she looked straight at me for a few seconds and hugged me tightly.*
Ichika: "I love you, Futaba."
Futaba: "Love you too..."
Ichika: "And I don't just mean as my twin sister. I'm in love with you, Futaba."
Futaba: "!"
*I blushed in embarrassment.*
*That was quite a thing to say to someone in the school infirmary.*
*Seeing Ichika, the girl who always completed her work with calm composure and cool perfection, getting all embarrassed over it made me even happier.*
Futaba: "Me too! I'm in love with you too, Ichika!"
*We told each other how we felt, and we hugged each other even tighter.*
*The setting sun had banded the sky in orange and indigo by the time Ichika and I headed home together.*
*I asked myself how long it had been since we'd last walked home together. A chilly evening breeze blew past, a sign of winter on the way, making me happy I had Ichika's warm hand in mine.*
*No, it wasn't the warmth of her hand that made me happy. I was happy just to be walking home together with Ichika again, at last.*
Futaba: "Ehehe."
Ichika: "What was that giggle for?"
Futaba: "Huh? I'm just happy I'm with you."
Ichika: "I see."
Futaba: "I've been all alone a lot recently."
Ichika: "I'm... sorry for that."
Futaba: "Don't worry about it. It's fine."
Ichika: "Really?"
Futaba: "Yeah. After all, we were able to tell each other how we feel. So now everything is fine!"
*I smiled at Ichika, and she smiled back happily.*
*But after that brief smile, a cloud settled over her, and she looked down in concern.*
Ichika: "Futaba, are you... okay now?"
Futaba: "Huh? What do you mean?"
Ichika: "Are you going to start eating again?"
Futaba: "Oh, yup! I'll eat like you wouldn't believe!"
Ichika: "I'm very glad to hear that, but you haven't been eating for a while, so you lost some weight. Make sure you pace yourself, alright?"
Futaba: "Yeah. The truth is, I've been so hungry at night I haven't been sleeping well."
*I declared with a giggle, and Ichika quietly apologized to me once more.*
*Ichika was so pretty. We were twins, sure, and we looked alike, but Ichika was really in a league of her own.*
*Maybe it had something to do with that aura of maturity she always had.*
*Apparently, while I'd been thinking to myself, Ichika had been doing the same. A moment later, she patted my head, smiling.*
Ichika: "Hey, Futaba?"
Futaba: "What?"
Ichika: "I really hurt you this time, didn't I?"
Futaba: "I told you, it's fine."
Ichika: "Thank you. But still, I have to make it up to you somehow."
Futaba: "What do you mean?"
Ichika: "I mean, it was my fault you ate so little you passed out. So I was thinking..."
Futaba: "What?"
Ichika: "Name any one thing you want from me, and I'll do it."
Futaba: "Whoa, really?!"
Ichika: "Yes. But you only get one wish, okay?"
*When I heard that lovely offer, I straight-up forgot that we were out in public.*
Futaba: "Yaaaay!"
Ichika: "Oh!"
*I embraced Ichika without thinking.*
Ichika: "Sheesh, come on, Futaba. Calm down a little."
*Ichika smiled and soothed me, but I just hugged her even tighter.*
*And then I made the wish that I'd been carrying with me for so long.*
Futaba: "Okay then, I want to go on a date together!"
*Although we'd already confirmed our love for each other, there were still so many feelings we hadn't yet been able to convey.*
